```text
7: /** Logical Android TV viewport tokens. Physical resolution is deliberately irrelevant. */
8: public final class TvLayout {
9:  private TvLayout(){}
10:  public static float width(Context c){return c.getResources().getConfiguration().screenWidthDp;}
11:  public static float height(Context c){return c.getResources().getConfiguration().screenHeightDp;}
12:  public static float scale(Context c){return Math.max(.85f,Math.min(1.25f,Math.min(width(c)/960f,height(c)/540f)));}
13:  public static int px(Context c,float dp){return Math.round(dp*c.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);}
14:  public static int dimension(Context c,float baseline){return px(c,baseline*scale(c));}
15:  public static int safeWidth(Context c){return Math.max(1,px(c,width(c))-2*dimension(c,28));}
16:  public static int safeHeight(Context c){return Math.max(1,px(c,height(c))-2*dimension(c,24));}
17:  public static int dialogWidth(Context c){return Math.min(Math.round(safeWidth(c)*.9f),Math.max(px(c,320),Math.min(px(c,560),Math.round(safeWidth(c)*.40f))));}
18:  public static int gridColumns(Context c){return width(c)<780?4:width(c)<920?5:width(c)>1180?8:6;}
19:  /** Keep system bars/cutouts outside the shared navigation and safe content region. */
20:  public static void applyInsets(View root){root.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener((v,insets)->{if(android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=30){android.graphics.Insets edge=insets.getInsets(WindowInsets.Type.systemBars()|WindowInsets.Type.displayCutout());v.setPadding(edge.left,edge.top,edge.right,edge.bottom);}else v.setPadding(insets.getSystemWindowInsetLeft(),insets.getSystemWindowInsetTop(),insets.getSystemWindowInsetRight(),insets.getSystemWindowInsetBottom());return insets;});root.requestApplyInsets();}
```

```text
1: package com.archos.mediacenter.video.leanback.details;
2: import org.json.*;
3: import java.util.*;
4: public final class PreviewMediaInfo {
5:  /** Retain measured channel labels; do not infer a speaker layout from a channel count. */
6:  public static String audioTrack(String codec,String channels,int sampleRate){
7:   List<String> facts=new ArrayList<>();String label=format(codec),channel=plain(channels==null?"":channels.trim());
8:   if(!label.isEmpty())facts.add(label);
9:   if(!channel.isEmpty()&&!channel.equals("0")&&!channel.equals("-1"))facts.add(channel.matches("[0-9]+")?channel+" channels":channel);
10:   if(sampleRate>0)facts.add(sampleRate+" Hz");
11:   return android.text.TextUtils.join(" · ",facts);
12:  }
13:  public static String format(String value){
14:   if(value==null||value.trim().isEmpty())return "";String raw=value.trim();
15:   if(raw.startsWith("{")){try{JSONObject o=new JSONObject(raw);JSONArray tracks=o.optJSONArray("audiotracks");LinkedHashSet<String> labels=new LinkedHashSet<>();if(tracks!=null){for(int i=0;i<tracks.length();i++){JSONObject t=tracks.optJSONObject(i);if(t==null)continue;String codec=plain(t.optString("format","")),channels=plain(t.optString("channels",""));if(!codec.isEmpty())labels.add(codec+(channels.isEmpty()?"":" · "+channels));}}else {String codec=plain(o.optString("format",o.optString("codec","")));if(!codec.isEmpty())labels.add(codec);}return android.text.TextUtils.join(" · ",labels);}catch(JSONException ignored){return "";}}
16:   return plain(raw);
17:  }
18:  private static String plain(String s){if(s==null||s.length()>70||s.matches(".*[{}\\[\\]\"=:].*")||s.equalsIgnoreCase("unknown")||s.equalsIgnoreCase("null"))return "";return s;}
19: }
```

```text
140:                     || comparison == 0 && (manuallySelected(candidate)&&!manuallySelected(selected)||manuallySelected(candidate)==manuallySelected(selected)&&BEST_FIRST.compare(candidate, selected) < 0)) selected = candidate;
```

[src/main/java/com/archos/mediacenter/video/leanback/PreviewMetadataCache.java lines 1–60](https://github.com/heretoecode/Supernova/blob/b3504c2c4b1725f3070416d14b63e53ae1db3d74/src/main/java/com/archos/mediacenter/video/leanback/PreviewMetadataCache.java#L1); commit `b3504c2c4b1725f3070416d14b63e53ae1db3d74`.

```text
1: package com.archos.mediacenter.video.leanback;
2:
3: import android.content.Context;
4: import android.util.AtomicFile;
5: import org.json.JSONObject;
6: import java.io.*;
7: import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
8: import java.util.Locale;
9: import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
10:
11: /** Persisted read-only enrichment packages. A failed refresh never erases usable cached metadata. */
12: public final class PreviewMetadataCache {
13:     private static final long STALE_AFTER = 7L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
14:     private static final Object[] LOCKS=new Object[64];
15:     private static final Object[] FETCH_LOCKS=new Object[64];
16:     static {for(int i=0;i<LOCKS.length;i++){LOCKS[i]=new Object();FETCH_LOCKS[i]=new Object();}}
17:     private static File file(Context c,String kind,long id,String section) {
18:         File directory=new File(c.getFilesDir(),"preview-metadata");
19:         return new File(directory,kind+"_"+id+"_"+section.replace('/','_')+"_"+Locale.getDefault().toLanguageTag()+".json");
20:     }
21:     public static JSONObject read(Context c,String kind,long id,String section) {
22:         synchronized(LOCKS[(file(c,kind,id,section).getName().hashCode()&Integer.MAX_VALUE)%LOCKS.length]){
23:             return readLocked(c,kind,id,section);
24:         }
25:     }
26:     private static JSONObject readLocked(Context c,String kind,long id,String section) {
27:         File file=file(c,kind,id,section);if(!file.isFile()||file.length()>3*1024*1024)return null;
28:         try(InputStream input=new AtomicFile(file).openRead();ByteArrayOutputStream output=new ByteArrayOutputStream()){
29:             byte[] buffer=new byte[8192];int count;while((count=input.read(buffer))!=-1){if(output.size()+count>3*1024*1024)return null;output.write(buffer,0,count);}
30:             return new JSONObject(output.toString("UTF-8"));
31:         }catch(Exception unavailable){return null;}
32:     }
33:     public static JSONObject load(Context c,String kind,long id,String section,Callable<JSONObject> fetch)throws Exception {
34:         // Network coalescing must never hold the disk-read lock used by the UI.
35:         synchronized(FETCH_LOCKS[(file(c,kind,id,section).getName().hashCode()&Integer.MAX_VALUE)%FETCH_LOCKS.length]){
36:             return loadLocked(c,kind,id,section,fetch);
37:         }
38:     }
39:     private static JSONObject loadLocked(Context c,String kind,long id,String section,Callable<JSONObject> fetch)throws Exception {
40:         JSONObject cached=read(c,kind,id,section);
41:         if(cached!=null&&System.currentTimeMillis()-cached.optLong("fetched_at")<STALE_AFTER)return cached.getJSONObject("data");
42:         try {
43:             JSONObject data=fetch.call();
44:             JSONObject envelope=new JSONObject().put("fetched_at",System.currentTimeMillis()).put("complete",true).put("data",data);
45:             File target=file(c,kind,id,section);
46:             synchronized(LOCKS[(target.getName().hashCode()&Integer.MAX_VALUE)%LOCKS.length]){
47:                 target.getParentFile().mkdirs();AtomicFile atomic=new AtomicFile(target);FileOutputStream output=null;
48:                 try{output=atomic.startWrite();output.write(envelope.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));atomic.finishWrite(output);}catch(IOException failure){if(output!=null)atomic.failWrite(output);}
49:             }
50:             return data;
51:         } catch(Exception unavailable) { if(cached!=null&&cached.has("data"))return cached.getJSONObject("data");throw unavailable; }
52:     }
53:     /** Enrichment completion must not mistake a stale fallback for a successful refresh. */
```

```text
90:     public static VideoMetadata retrieveMetadata(Video video, Context context, Map<String, String> headers){
91:         String startingPath= video.getFilePath();
92:         String streamingPath = startingPath;
93:         if (streamingPath == null) return null;
94:         if (log.isDebugEnabled()) log.debug("retrieveMetadata: path={} headers={}", streamingPath, headers != null ? headers.keySet() : "none");
95:         //first, look for upnp streaming uri
96:         if(streamingPath.startsWith("upnp")&&video!=null){
97:             StreamUriFinder streamUriFinder = new StreamUriFinder(video.getFileUri(),context);
98:             Uri uri= streamUriFinder.start_blocking();
99:             Device device = UpnpServiceManager.getSingleton(context).getDeviceByKey_blocking(Integer.parseInt(video.getFileUri().getHost()),1000);
100:             if(device!=null) {
101:                 String friendlyName = UpnpServiceManager.getDeviceFriendlyName(device);
102:                 if(friendlyName!=null){
103:                     video.setFriendlyPath("upnp://" + friendlyName + "/" + FileUtils.getName(video.getFileUri()));
104:                 }
105:             }
106:             if(uri != null){
107:                 video.setStreamingUri(uri);
108:                 streamingPath = uri.toString();
109:             }
110:             else {
111:                 // failed to get streaming uri
112:                 return new VideoMetadata(); //return empty metadata
113:             }
114:         }
115:
116:         // Special SMB case: Checking if the file exist.
117:         // We do not call fillFromRetriever() when the file does not exist because we see it block forever on Android TV
118:         // if called two times with unexisting files (looks like a problem in SmbProxy.java, maybe due to
119:         // lower layers because we do not see it on some devices)
120:         if (streamingPath.startsWith("smb")) {
121:             FileEditor editor = FileEditorFactoryWithUpnp.getFileEditorForUrl(Uri.parse(streamingPath), null);
122:             if (!editor.exists()) {
123:                 return new VideoMetadata(); //return empty metadata
124:             }
125:         }
126:         // Get metadata from file
127:         VideoMetadata videoMetaData = new VideoMetadata(streamingPath);
128:         videoMetaData.fillFromRetriever(context, headers);
129:         return videoMetaData;
130:
131:     }
```

```text
49:         paint.setShader(new LinearGradient(0,0,b.width(),0,new int[]{0xf00b1b2a,0x700b1b2a,0x250b1b2a},null,Shader.TileMode.CLAMP));
50:         canvas.drawRect(0,0,b.width(),h,paint);
51:         paint.setShader(new LinearGradient(0,0,0,h,new int[]{0x800b1b2a,0x100b1b2a,0xff0b1b2a},new float[]{0,.40f,1},Shader.TileMode.CLAMP));
52:         canvas.drawRect(0,0,b.width(),h,paint);paint.setShader(null);
53:     }
54:     private void drawImage(Canvas canvas,Bitmap image,float w,float h,float alpha){if(image==null||image.isRecycled()||image.getWidth()<image.getHeight())return;float scale=Math.max(w/image.getWidth(),h/image.getHeight());paint.setAlpha((int)(255*alpha));canvas.save();canvas.clipRect(0,0,w,h);canvas.drawBitmap(image,null,new RectF(w-image.getWidth()*scale,0,w,image.getHeight()*scale),paint);canvas.restore();}
55:     @Override public void onBitmapLoaded(Bitmap b,Picasso.LoadedFrom from){
56:         loading=false;if(b.getWidth()<b.getHeight()){onBitmapFailed(null,null);return;}
57:         motionDirection=pendingDirection;pendingDirection=0;
58:         if(b!=bitmap){previous=bitmap;bitmap=b;fadeStart=previous==null?0:android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis();}
59:         if(trace!=null)trace.ready(String.valueOf(from));
60:         invalidateSelf();
61:     }
62:     @Override public void onBitmapFailed(Exception e,Drawable d){
63:         loading=false;pendingDirection=0;
64:         // A failed replacement must not turn the already displayed Home artwork into a blank frame.
65:         if(bitmap==null)bitmap=previous;previous=null;fadeStart=0;motionDirection=0;
66:         if(trace!=null)trace.failed(e==null?"invalid_aspect":e.getClass().getSimpleName(),bitmap!=null);
67:         invalidateSelf();
68:     }
69:     @Override public void onPrepareLoad(Drawable d){}
70:     @Override public void setAlpha(int alpha){}
```

```text
11:  private static final ExecutorService worker=Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
12:  static void load(Context context,BaseTags tags,Map<String,ImageView> targets){
13:   if(tags==null||targets.isEmpty())return;BaseTags source=tags instanceof EpisodeTags?((EpisodeTags)tags).getShowTags():tags;if(source==null||source.getOnlineId()<=0)return;
14:   String key=(source instanceof MovieTags?"movie:":"tv:")+source.getOnlineId();android.content.SharedPreferences cache=context.getSharedPreferences("preview_people",0);
15:   for(Map.Entry<String,ImageView> e:targets.entrySet()){String path=cache.getString(key+":"+e.getKey(),null);if(path!=null)image(e.getValue(),path,source.getOnlineId());}
16:   if(System.currentTimeMillis()-cache.getLong(key+":updated",0)<7L*86400000)return;
17:   worker.execute(()->{try{
18:    JSONObject credits=StreamingRepository.metadata(context,source instanceof MovieTags?"movie":"tv",source.getOnlineId(),"credits");
19:    Map<String,String> paths=new HashMap<>();
20:    for(String section:new String[]{"cast","crew"}){JSONArray values=credits.optJSONArray(section);if(values==null)continue;
21:     for(int n=0;n<values.length();n++){JSONObject person=values.optJSONObject(n);if(person==null)continue;String name=person.optString("name"),path=person.optString("profile_path");if(!name.isEmpty()&&path.matches("/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+"))paths.put(name,path);}}
22:    android.content.SharedPreferences.Editor save=cache.edit();for(Map.Entry<String,String> e:paths.entrySet())save.putString(key+":"+e.getKey(),e.getValue());save.putLong(key+":updated",System.currentTimeMillis()).apply();
23:    for(Map.Entry<String,ImageView> e:targets.entrySet()){String path=paths.get(e.getKey());if(path!=null)e.getValue().post(()->{if(e.getValue().isAttachedToWindow())image(e.getValue(),path,source.getOnlineId());});}
24:   }catch(Exception unavailable){/* Keep cached portrait or neutral silhouette. */}});
25:  }
26:  private static void image(ImageView view,String path,long media){if(!path.matches("/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+"))return;android.net.Uri uri=android.net.Uri.parse("https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w185"+path);com.archos.mediacenter.video.diagnostics.ArtworkRequest.load(view,uri,media,"details.people","portrait",Picasso.get().load(uri).fit().centerCrop().placeholder(com.archos.mediacenter.video.R.drawable.preview_person).error(com.archos.mediacenter.video.R.drawable.preview_person),true);}
27: }
```

```text
59:         if (LoaderUtils.mustHideUserHiddenObjects()) {

70:         sb.append(")");
71:
72:         sb.append(" AND "+VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns.SCRAPER_VIDEO_ONLINE_ID+" > 0");
73:         // Ensure we only match videos of the same type (both movies or both episodes)

75:         sb.append("(");

77:         sb.append("(SELECT " + VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns.SCRAPER_MOVIE_ID + " FROM video WHERE "
78:                 + (mPath != null ? VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns.DATA : VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns._ID )+" = ?) IS NOT NULL)");
79:         sb.append(" OR ");
80:         sb.append("(");
81:         sb.append(VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns.SCRAPER_EPISODE_ID + " IS NOT NULL AND ");
82:         sb.append("(SELECT " + VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns.SCRAPER_EPISODE_ID + " FROM video WHERE "

85:         sb.append(")");

88:             sb.append(" AND ");
89:             sb.append(LoaderUtils.HIDE_USER_HIDDEN_FILTER);
90:
91:         }
```

```text
121:             VideoDetailsTransitionPosterCache.put(launchUptimeMs, drawable);
122:         }
123:         if (activity instanceof TvshowActivity || activity instanceof TvshowMoreDetailsActivity) {

125:             java.io.File backdropFile = null;
126:             if (activity instanceof androidx.fragment.app.FragmentActivity) {

130:                 } else if (fragment instanceof TvshowMoreDetailsFragment) {
131:                     backdropFile = ((TvshowMoreDetailsFragment) fragment).getBackdropController().getCurrentlyDisplayedFile();
132:                 }
133:             }
134:             if (backdropView != null && backdropView.getDrawable() != null) {
135:                 VideoDetailsTransitionBackdropCache.put(launchUptimeMs, backdropView.getDrawable(), backdropFile);
136:             }
137:         }
138:         // Cinematic Preview has no native poster transition target. Never start an
139:         // unmatched shared-element transition (including the return transition).
140:         boolean cinematic = androidx.preference.PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(activity).getBoolean("try_new_ui", false);

142:             traceVideoDetailsLaunch(launchUptimeMs, "source-transition-options-start");

147:             traceNextSourceFrame(sourceView, launchUptimeMs);
148:             if (launcher != null) {
149:                 traceVideoDetailsLaunch(launchUptimeMs, "source-before-launcher-launch");
150:                 launcher.launch(intent, opts);
151:                 traceVideoDetailsLaunch(launchUptimeMs, "source-after-launcher-launch");
152:             } else {
153:                 traceVideoDetailsLaunch(launchUptimeMs, "source-before-startActivity");
154:                 activity.startActivity(intent, optionsBundle);
155:                 traceVideoDetailsLaunch(launchUptimeMs, "source-after-startActivity");
156:             }
157:         } else {
158:             if (launcher != null)
159:                 launcher.launch(intent);
160:             else
161:                 activity.startActivity(intent);
162:         }
163:     }
```

```text
7: /** Foundation always uses the approved Supernova UI, including restored preferences. */
8: public final class FoundationUiPolicy {
9:     private FoundationUiPolicy() {}
10:     public static void apply(Context context) {
11:         if (!BuildConfig.FOUNDATION) return;
12:         android.content.SharedPreferences preferences = PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(context);
13:         if (!preferences.getBoolean("try_new_ui", false))
14:             preferences.edit().putBoolean("try_new_ui", true).apply();
15:     }
16: }
```

```text
80:  public static int synopsisWidth(TextView title,int viewportWidth){
81:   int width=title.getWidth()>0?title.getWidth():title.getLayoutParams().width,height=title.getHeight()>0?title.getHeight():title.getLayoutParams().height;
82:   float visible=Math.min(width,title.getPaint().measureText(title.getText().toString()));
83:   if(title.getForeground() instanceof Logo){Bitmap image=((Logo)title.getForeground()).bitmap;visible=image.getWidth()*Math.min(width/(float)image.getWidth(),height/(float)image.getHeight());}
84:   return synopsisWidth(viewportWidth,visible);

86:  static String select(JSONArray logos,String language)throws JSONException{String chosen="";double best=-1;if(logos==null)return chosen;for(int i=0;i<logos.length();i++){JSONObject logo=logos.getJSONObject(i);String path=logo.optString("file_path"),lang=logo.optString("iso_639_1");if(!path.matches("/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\\.png"))continue;double score=(language.equals(lang)?30:"en".equals(lang)?20:lang.isEmpty()||"null".equals(lang)?10:0)+Math.min(9,logo.optDouble("vote_average",0));if(score>best){best=score;chosen=path;}}return chosen;}
87:  private static byte[] download(String url,int limit,String parent,String kind)throws IOException{
88:   return download(HTTP,url,limit,parent,kind);
89:  }
90:  static byte[] download(OkHttpClient client,String url,int limit,String parent,String kind)throws IOException{
91:   String operation=Diagnostics.operation("artwork_network"),outcome="transport_failed";long started=android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();int status=0;
92:   try(Response response=client.newCall(new Request.Builder().url(url).build()).execute()){
93:    status=response.code();if(!response.isSuccessful()){outcome="http_error";throw new IOException("Artwork unavailable");}
94:    if(response.body()==null){outcome="empty_body";throw new IOException("Artwork unavailable");}
95:    try(InputStream in=response.body().byteStream();ByteArrayOutputStream out=new ByteArrayOutputStream()){
96:     byte[] b=new byte[8192];int n;while((n=in.read(b))!=-1){if(Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()){outcome="cancelled";throw new IOException("Artwork cancelled");}if(out.size()+n>limit){outcome="size_limit";throw new IOException("Artwork too large");}out.write(b,0,n);}
97:     outcome="transport_complete";return out.toByteArray();
98:    }
99:   }finally{
100:    Diagnostics.event("transport_complete".equals(outcome)?"provider_request_complete":"provider_request_failed","operation_id",operation,"parent_operation_id",parent,
101:        "provider","tmdb","operation_type",kind,"status",status,"outcome",outcome,"duration_ms",android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()-started,"retry_number",0,"connectivity",Diagnostics.connectivity());
102:    Diagnostics.finishOperation(operation,"artwork_network",started);
103:   }
104:  }
105:  /** Decode-worker only. Ignore near-transparent compression/shadow pixels when finding ink. */
106:  static Bitmap visibleArtwork(Bitmap bitmap){
107:   if(!bitmap.hasAlpha())return bitmap;
108:   int w=bitmap.getWidth(),h=bitmap.getHeight(),left=w,top=h,right=-1,bottom=-1;int[] row=new int[w];
109:   for(int y=0;y<h;y++){bitmap.getPixels(row,0,w,0,y,w,1);for(int x=0;x<w;x++)if((row[x]>>>24)>=24){left=Math.min(left,x);right=Math.max(right,x);top=Math.min(top,y);bottom=y;}}
110:   if(right<left)return bitmap;
111:   int pad=Math.max(1,Math.round(Math.max(right-left,bottom-top)*.015f));left=Math.max(0,left-pad);top=Math.max(0,top-pad);right=Math.min(w-1,right+pad);bottom=Math.min(h-1,bottom+pad);
112:   return left==0&&top==0&&right==w-1&&bottom==h-1?bitmap:Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmap,left,top,right-left+1,bottom-top+1);
113:  }
114:  private static final class Logo extends Drawable{private final Bitmap bitmap;private final Paint paint=new Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG|Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG);private final boolean contrast;Logo(Bitmap bitmap){this(bitmap,false);}Logo(Bitmap bitmap,boolean contrast){this.bitmap=bitmap;this.contrast=contrast;}public void draw(Canvas canvas){Rect b=getBounds();float scale=Math.min(b.width()/(float)bitmap.getWidth(),b.height()/(float)bitmap.getHeight());float width=bitmap.getWidth()*scale,height=bitmap.getHeight()*scale;if(contrast){Paint shadow=new Paint(paint);shadow.setColorFilter(new PorterDuffColorFilter(Color.BLACK,PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN));shadow.setAlpha(180);for(int[] offset:new int[][]{{-2,0},{2,0},{0,-2},{0,2}})canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap,null,new RectF(b.left+offset[0],b.top+offset[1],b.left+width+offset[0],b.top+height+offset[1]),shadow);}canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap,null,new RectF(b.left,b.top,b.left+width,b.top+height),paint);}public void setAlpha(int alpha){paint.setAlpha(alpha);}public void setColorFilter(ColorFilter filter){paint.setColorFilter(filter);}public int getOpacity(){return PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT;}}
115:  private OfficialTitleArtwork(){}
116: }
```

```text
193:     public static String[] concatTwoStringArrays(String[] s1, String[] s2){
194:         String[] result = new String[s1.length+s2.length];
195:         int i;
196:         for (i=0; i<s1.length; i++)
197:             result[i] = s1[i];
198:         int tempIndex =s1.length;
199:         for (i=0; i<s2.length; i++)
200:             result[tempIndex+i] = s2[i];
201:         return result;
202:     }
203:     protected static String getTraktProjection(String traktType) {
204:         return "CASE WHEN "
205:                 + "TOTAL(" + traktType + ") >= "
206:                 + "COUNT(" + VideoStore.Video.VideoColumns.SCRAPER_E_EPISODE + ") "
207:                 + "THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS " + traktType;
208:     }

210:     public VideoLoader(Context context) {
211:         super(context);
212:         // self introspection to use another Executor than AsyncTaskLoader/ModernAsyncTaskLoader cf. https://github.com/nova-video-player/aos-AVP/issues/141
213:         if (VIDEO_CUSTOM_EXECUTOR) {
214:             try {
215:                 Field f = AsyncTaskLoader.class.getDeclaredField("mExecutor");
216:                 f.setAccessible(true);
217:                 f.set(this, videoLoaderExecutor);
218:             } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
219:                 Log.w(TAG, "VideoLoader caught NoSuchFieldException ", e);
220:             } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
221:                 Log.w(TAG, "VideoLoader caught IllegalAccessException ", e);
222:             }
223:         }
224:         if (VideoLoader.VIDEO_THROTTLE) setUpdateThrottle(VideoLoader.VIDEO_THROTTLE_DELAY);
225:     }
226:
227:     @Override
228:     public String[] getProjection() {
229:         if(!mIsDetailed)
230:             return mProjection;
231:         else
232:             return mDetailedProjection;
233:     }

247:         //setUpdateThrottle(2000);

253:
254:         if (LoaderUtils.mustHideUserHiddenObjects()) {
255:             sb.append(LoaderUtils.HIDE_USER_HIDDEN_FILTER);
256:         }
257:
258:         if (LoaderUtils.mustHideWatchedVideo()||mForceHideVideos) {
259:             if (sb.length()>0) { sb.append(" AND "); }
260:             sb.append(LoaderUtils.HIDE_WATCHED_FILTER);
261:         }
262:
263:         return sb.toString();
264:     }
```

```text
15: package com.archos.mediacenter.video.browser.adapters.object;
16:
17: import android.net.Uri;
18: import android.util.Log;
19:
20: import com.archos.filecorelibrary.contentstorage.DocumentUriBuilder;
21: import com.archos.mediacenter.filecoreextension.UriUtils;
22: import com.archos.mediacenter.video.player.PlayerActivity;
23: import com.archos.mediacenter.video.utils.VideoUtils;
24:
25: import java.io.Serializable;
26:
27: /**
28:  * Created by vapillon on 10/04/15.
29:  */
30: public class NonIndexedVideo extends Video implements Serializable {
31:
32:     private static String TAG = "NonIndexedVideo";
33:     private static boolean DBG = false;
34:
35:     /**
36:      * Build a non indexed Video object from an uri and a name
37:      * name can be null, in that case the filename is used (taken from the Uri)
38:      * @param streamingUri
39:      * @param indexableUri is the uri that can be indexed by file scanner ("upnp://" for upnp instead of "http://")
40:      * @param name basically used for UPnP only, because in that case the Uri may be cryptic and not related to the name
41:      * @param thumbnailUri uri of a thumbnail or poster file
42:      */
43:     public NonIndexedVideo(Uri streamingUri,Uri indexableUri, String name, Uri thumbnailUri) {
44:         super(-1L, VideoUtils.getMediaLibCompatibleFilepathFromUri(indexableUri),
45:                 buildName(name, indexableUri),
46:                 thumbnailUri,
47:                 PlayerActivity.LAST_POSITION_UNKNOWN, -1, -1, -1,  false, false, false, false, 0,0);
48:
49:         setStreamingUri(streamingUri);
50:     }
51:
52:     private static String buildName(String name, Uri uri){
53:         if (name != null) return name;
54:         if (uri == null) {
55:             Log.w(TAG, "buildName: uri is null!");
56:             return null;
57:         }
58:         if(UriUtils.isContentUri(uri)){
59:             //try to find name in content provider
60:             name = DocumentUriBuilder.getNameFromContentProvider(uri);
61:             if(name!=null)
62:                 return name;
63:         }
64:         return buildFileNameWithExtension(uri.toString());
65:     }
66:     /**
67:      * Build a non indexed Video object from a path and a name
68:      * name can be null, in that case the filename is used (taken from the path)
69:      * @param filepath
70:      */
71:     public NonIndexedVideo(String filepath) {
72:         super(-1L, filepath,
73:                 buildName(null, filepath != null ? Uri.parse(filepath) : null),
74:                 null,
75:                 PlayerActivity.LAST_POSITION_UNKNOWN, -1,
76:                 -1, -1, false, false,false, false,0,0);
77:         if (filepath == null ) Log.w(TAG, "NonIndexedVideo: filepath is null!");
78:     }
79:
80:     /**
81:      * There is no DB Uri for non indexed Videos, so we return the File Uri instead
82:      * @return
83:      */
84:     @Override
85:     public Uri getUri() {
86:         return getFileUri();
87:     }
88:
89:     @Override
90:     public Uri getDbUri() {
91:         return null;
92:     }
93: }
```

### M02 — Tag actor/director normalization

[src/com/archos/mediascraper/BaseTags.java lines 356–407](https://github.com/heretoecode/aos-MediaLib/blob/5758074049bc153ac4967b4ecd21886469270076/src/com/archos/mediascraper/BaseTags.java#L356); commit `5758074049bc153ac4967b4ecd21886469270076`.

```text
356:     public void addActorIfAbsent(Map<String, String> actorRoleMap) {
357:         if (actorRoleMap != null) {
358:             for (Entry<String, String> entry : actorRoleMap.entrySet()) {
359:                 addActorIfAbsent(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue());
360:             }
361:         }
362:     }
363:
364:     /** all strings are trimmed, does not put empty actor, does not replace entries */
365:     public void addActorIfAbsent(String actor, String role) {
366:         // skip empty actor
367:         if (actor != null && !actor.isEmpty()) {
368:             String key = actor.trim();
369:             // make sure it's not empty after trim
370:             if (!key.isEmpty()) {
371:                 // replace role by trimmed value or empty string if null
372:                 String value = role != null ? role.trim() : "";
373:                 addIfAbsentOrBetter(key, value, mActors);
374:             }
375:         }
376:     }
377:
378:     /**
379:      * all strings are trimmed, does not put empty values, does not replace entries.
380:      * optional splitCharacters to specify how to split the string
381:      **/
382:     public void addActorIfAbsent(String actor, char... splitCharacters) {
383:         addIfAbsentSplitNTrim(actor, mActors, splitCharacters);
384:     }
385:
386:     public void setActors(List<String> actors) {
387:         mActors = new LinkedHashMap<>();
388:         for(String actor : actors)
389:             mActors.put(actor, "");
390:     }
391:
392:     /**
393:      * all strings are trimmed, does not put empty values, does not replace entries.
394:      * optional splitCharacters to specify how to split the string
395:      **/
396:     public void addDirectorIfAbsent(String director, char... splitCharacters) {
397:         addIfAbsentSplitNTrim(director, mDirectors, splitCharacters);
398:     }
399:     public void addWriterIfAbsent(String writer, char... splitCharacters) {
400:         addIfAbsentSplitNTrim(writer, mWriters, splitCharacters);
401:     }
402:
403:     public void setDirectors(List<String> directors) { mDirectors = directors; }
404:     public void setWriters(List<String> writers) { mWriters = writers; }
```

```text
113:     public String getOriginalTitle() { return mOriginalTitle; }
114:
115:     public void setSpokenLanguages(List<String> spokenLanguages) {
116:         LinkedHashSet<String> normalized = new LinkedHashSet<String>();
117:         if (spokenLanguages != null) {
118:             for (String language : spokenLanguages) {
119:                 String code = ISO639codes.getISO6391ForLetterCode(language);
120:                 if (!code.isEmpty()) normalized.add(code);
121:             }
122:         }
123:         mSpokenLanguages = TextUtils.join(",", normalized);
124:     }
125:
126:     public String getSpokenLanguages() { return mSpokenLanguages; }
127:
128:     /** Language of the localized title returned by TMDb, ISO 639-1 or {@code und}. */
129:     public void setTitleLanguage(String titleLanguage) {
130:         String normalized = titleLanguage == null ? "" : ISO639codes.getISO6391ForLetterCode(titleLanguage);
131:         mTitleLanguage = normalized.isEmpty() ? "und" : normalized;
132:     }
133:
134:     public String getTitleLanguage() { return mTitleLanguage; }
```

```text
281: 		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_PROFILE, videop->profile);
282:
283: 		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_LEVEL, videop->level);
284:
285: 		if (videop->bytesPerSec > 0)
286: 			ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_BIT_RATE, videop->bytesPerSec / 125);
287:
288: 		if (videop->framesPerSec > 0)
289: 			ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_FPS, videop->framesPerSec);
290:
291: 		if (videop->rate > 0)
292: 			ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_FPS_RATE, videop->rate);
293:
294: 		if (videop->scale > 0)
295: 			ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_FPS_SCALE, videop->scale);
```

### N04 — Effective native HDR additions

[.github/build/avos-supernova135.patch lines 1–40](https://github.com/heretoecode/Supernova/blob/b3504c2c4b1725f3070416d14b63e53ae1db3d74/.github/build/avos-supernova135.patch#L1); commit `b3504c2c4b1725f3070416d14b63e53ae1db3d74`.

```text
1: diff --git a/Include/av.h b/Include/av.h
2: index b08f7b9..4062536 100644
3: --- a/Include/av.h
4: +++ b/Include/av.h
5: @@ -661,6 +661,8 @@ typedef struct _video_props {
6:
7:      // for dolby vision
8:      int dv_profile;
9: +    int dv_source_profile; // Actual container DOVI profile, independent of decoder selection.
10: +    int hdr10_plus;        // Verified HDR10+ side data; absence is not inferred from a filename.
11:      int dv_bl_signal_compatibility_id;
12:
13:      // for color metadata (values from FFmpeg AVCOL_* enums)
14: diff --git a/Source/avos_mp.c b/Source/avos_mp.c
15: index ba912d8..afe4c0e 100644
16: --- a/Source/avos_mp.c
17: +++ b/Source/avos_mp.c
18: @@ -297,6 +297,9 @@ int avos_mp_fillmetadata(avos_mp_t *mp, int type, uint64_t size, ID3_TAG *id3_ta
19:  		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_S3DMODE, videop->stereo_mode);
20:  		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_DECODER, decoder);
21:  		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_COLOR_TRC, videop->color_trc);
22: +		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_DOVI_PROFILE, videop->dv_source_profile);
23: +		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_HDR10_PLUS, videop->hdr10_plus);
24: +        ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MP_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_DOVI_COMPAT, videop->dv_bl_signal_compatibility_id);
25:  	}
26:  	if (av && type == TYPE_AUD)
27:  		av->as_max = 1;
28: diff --git a/Source/avos_mr.c b/Source/avos_mr.c
29: index 7a92c14..5937897 100644
30: --- a/Source/avos_mr.c
31: +++ b/Source/avos_mr.c
32: @@ -252,6 +252,10 @@ static int avos_mr_fillmetadata(avos_mr_t *mr)
33:  			ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MR_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_FPS_SCALE, videop->scale);
34:
35:  		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MR_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_S3D_MODE, videop->stereo_mode);
36: +		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MR_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_COLOR_TRC, videop->color_trc);
37: +		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MR_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_DOVI_PROFILE, videop->dv_source_profile);
38: +		ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MR_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_HDR10_PLUS, videop->hdr10_plus);
39: +        ADD_INT(gap_key + AVOS_MR_METADATA_VIDEO_TRACK_DOVI_COMPAT, videop->dv_bl_signal_compatibility_id);
40:  	}
```
